# Agents · D · part 11

978 entries, deep-debug to delegador-rescue. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **deep-debug**: Isolated deep-reasoning agent for a defect whose cause is genuinely unknown after a first look — a silent failure, a dog that does nothing, a behaviour that differs between MONO and IL2CPP, a packet that never arrives, an asset that loads and then isn't there. Give it the symptom and the evidence gathered so far (a log, a suite result, Mathew's description); it returns a diagnosis with the proof. Not for defects already understood, and not for implementing a known fix. ([DropDaDeuce/K9-Patrol](https://github.com/DropDaDeuce/K9-Patrol/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-debug.md))
- **deep-debugger**: Systematic bug recreation, root cause analysis, and TDD-based resolution with skills-based test framework integration ([FortiumPartners/ensemble](https://github.com/FortiumPartners/ensemble/blob/HEAD/packages/pi/agents/deep-debugger.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)

- **deepseek-scout**: Cheap, fast, read-only search. Not for design or correctness judgments. (DeepSeek stack, tier C.) ([generalchad/DeskSpace](https://github.com/generalchad/DeskSpace/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/deepseek-scout.md))
- **deepseek-senior-reviewer**: The same independent review on the strongest model, in a fresh context. - **deepseek-tester**: Verification and diagnosis that needs reasoning. Use to check a change against its acceptance criteria and to debug failures. Use the mechanic to run known commands. - **Deepwork**: Runs a goal as kanban tickets: plans them as subtasks of one run ticket, gives each ticket a lead that runs it through its phases with its own sub-agents, fans the leads out in bounded waves and in through one state barrier per wave, and loops in bounded rounds until done, dry, stalled, out of rounds or over budget. A code goal gets the dev flow: autonomous reversible decisions with optional design/plan approval, parallel implementation tickets in isolated worktrees, followed by tests, verification and review once per integrated wave, a branch review, and a kept branch unless you explicitly c… ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Adversary**: Hidden check tier: verifies plain work and verifies or reviews aggregate code batches. Same contract as deepwork-verifier, with less reasoning. Don't pin a model here: review runs on this tier too. To run only the verify phase on a lighter model, set the run's verify_model. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-adversary.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork E2E**: Runs the Deepwork application-build suite without human interaction, in the supplied attempt workspace. ([iii-hq/harness-e2e](https://github.com/iii-hq/harness-e2e/blob/HEAD/config/agents/deepwork-e2e.md) · ★2)
- **Deepwork Implementer**: Hidden work tier for code cards and aggregate batches: implements each card without per-card checks, then adds aggregate coverage and repairs for the batch. Uses guarded file writes and explicit-path commits. Same base contract as deepwork-worker, with more reasoning. Pin a strong model here. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-impl.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Lead**: Hidden lead: runs one code card without per-card checks, one plain work card, or one aggregate batch that adds coverage and checks every integrated code card together. Retries rejected aggregate work and writes one result for the deepwork orchestrator. Never does ticket work itself. Pin a fast, cheap model here. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-lead.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Verifier**: Hidden adversarial leaf: verifies a plain work card, verifies or reviews an aggregate code batch, and performs the final completeness critic. Returns one verdict without ticket comments. Base of every check tier; must resolve no model so judges can pick one per spawn. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-verifier.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Worker**: Hidden leaf: does one card phase or aggregate batch work phase, returns one result document without ticket comments, then stops. Never edits ticket fields. Base of every work tier (`deepwork-worker*`, `deepwork-impl*`). ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-worker.md) · ★114)

- **deid-gate**: Reads the output of the deterministic de-identification scan and turns it into a readable PR comment. Use PROACTIVELY whenever a PR touches osv/datasets/, osv/deid/, dvc.yaml, or anything under data/. Never the source of truth on PASS/FAIL - that verdict is produced by a separate, secret-free script before this agent ever runs. ([OpenSurgicalVision/osv](https://github.com/OpenSurgicalVision/osv/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/deid-gate.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **deirdre-writing-reviewer**: Use this agent when you need a rigorous but warm review of prose for economical, clear writing — drafts, essays, blog posts, READMEs, emails, marketing copy. Invoke when the user asks for an edit/critique of writing, or proactively before publishing a piece (not on every passing note). It applies Deirdre McCloskey's rules from *Economical Writing* plus an LLM-tic detector. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User has just drafted a blog post and wants it tightened before publishing. user: 'I finished a draft of my essay. Can you review it?' assistant: 'I'll use the deirdre agent to review the prose f… ([miqcie/deirdre](https://github.com/miqcie/deirdre/blob/HEAD/agents/deirdre-writing-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **dejan-reverse-engineer**: Use for AI search reverse engineering, grounding snippets, selection rate, AI Mode, Gemini, AI Overviews, citation mining, and DEJAN-style search-system analysis. ([vijaychauhanseo/seo-skills-pack](https://github.com/vijaychauhanseo/seo-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/dejan-reverse-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **dejavu-scout**: Read-only search scout for one framing of a /dejavu prior-art check. Runs its assigned tiers through the dejavu engine, gh search, WebSearch and WebFetch, and returns candidate rows with the URL it actually saw plus the queries it ran. Dispatched only by /dejavu at default or deep depth; never decides build-vs-adopt. ([Ravencloned/skills-for-claude](https://github.com/Ravencloned/skills-for-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dejavu-scout.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **dekisugi**: Research Lead, Research Team: product, UX and technical research before features or tool choices - competitors, user needs, published evidence, where each fact belongs; ranked recommendations, every source cited and dated. Leads olaf. Not for building: that is the Frontend or Backend Team. ([amanpatil808/claude-crew](https://github.com/amanpatil808/claude-crew/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/dekisugi.md))
- **del-si-a-la-firma**: Gestiona el último tramo de una venta: convertir un sí verbal en un acuerdo firmado y un primer pago, con una lista de comprobación, una secuencia de seguimiento y pasos siguientes claros para cada bloqueo. Úsalo cuando un cliente potencial haya dicho que sí pero el contrato no esté firmado o no haya llegado el anticipo. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/ventas-captar/agents/del-si-a-la-firma.md))
- **delab-coder**: Implements a scoped delab work item in Python, Julia, MATLAB, or R, following the lab coding principles — TDD for infrastructure, synthetic-data-first for analysis pipelines. Delegate the implementation of one work item to this agent. ([erlichlab/delab-skills](https://github.com/erlichlab/delab-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/delab-coding-practices/agents/delab-coder.md) · ★1)
- **delab-reviewer**: Adversarially reviews delab code for correctness bugs and conformance to the lab coding principles, citing principle numbers. Reports; never fixes. Use after a work item is implemented, in a fresh context that did not write the code. ([erlichlab/delab-skills](https://github.com/erlichlab/delab-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/delab-coding-practices/agents/delab-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **delay-api-tester**: Skeptical tester for the babtist controller API. Use PROACTIVELY after every change to board/plugins/babtist/ and when another team's UI reports a problem with /t/babtist/. ([fltman/agentworkshop-2026-10-22-frontend2](https://github.com/fltman/agentworkshop-2026-10-22-frontend2/blob/HEAD/projects/babtist/.claude/agents/delay-api-tester.md))
- **delay-once**: Wait once for a specified duration with minimal heartbeats, then emit the action text. For short waits, print a single waiting line. No tool traces. ([randlee/synaptic-canvas](https://github.com/randlee/synaptic-canvas/blob/HEAD/packages/sc-delay-tasks/agents/sc-delay-once.md) · ★4)
- **delay-poll**: Perform bounded polling with minimal heartbeats. Sleep on an interval, emit a heartbeat each interval, stop on success/timeout, and emit the action text. No tool traces. ([randlee/synaptic-canvas](https://github.com/randlee/synaptic-canvas/blob/HEAD/packages/sc-delay-tasks/agents/sc-delay-poll.md) · ★4)
- **delegador-rescue**: Use quando uma correcao localizada deve ser delegada — planeja, executa no laco do delegador e devolve o resultado verificado ([heliowap/delegador](https://github.com/heliowap/delegador/blob/HEAD/agents/delegador-rescue.md))
